KJV-MWB — Personalised Scripture Review Catalogue

Source translation: KJV (public domain). Catalogue 2026.11-d1, generator rule v1. Generated from exports/personalisation-review/review_records.json.

Nothing in this document is approved. “Automated checks passed” is a technical result, not a theological approval. Approval is recorded only with the reviewer’s name and date, for the exact proposal version.

How to review

PurposeThis workbook lists every proposed personalised rendering of Scripture in MY WORD BIBLE, and how every canonical verse of each translation was classified. Nothing in it is approved. A proposal is approved only when a named human reviewer records an approval for its current version.
Translations coveredKJV-MWB (from the KJV, public domain), catalogue 2026.11-d1; WEB-MWB (from the WEB, public domain), catalogue 2026.11-d1. No other translation is covered: NKJV, NIV and NLT are not licensed for derivative works and are excluded from every count.
Mode A — name in the ScriptureThe reader’s name is inserted as a direct address set off by commas. No source word is removed, replaced or re-cased and no pronoun changes. [Name] shows where the name appears (full, first or preferred name, as the reader chooses).
Mode B — name in the titleThe Scripture is shown exactly as written. Only the editorial title carries the reader’s first (or preferred) name. Many Mode B proposals still need an editorial title; they cannot pass automated validation until one is supplied.
Mode C — originalEvery verse without an approved proposal is shown unchanged. The Daily Word then uses the title “Daily Word – MWB”.
Automated validationA technical check (anchors, exact recovery, punctuation, pronoun person/number, verb agreement, capitalisation, deleted words, gendered terms, vocabulary). Passing is NOT a theological approval.
How to record a decisionUse the “Review decisions” sheet (or review_decisions_template.csv). Keep proposal_id and proposal_version exactly as given. In “decision” write one of: approve, reject, correct, comment, flag, reopen.
approveRequires reviewer name and date. Only possible for a proposal that passes automated validation at the given version.
rejectRequires a reason in “comments”.
correctRequires reviewer name. Write the full corrected verse in corrected_text using {name} where the name goes (Mode A), and/or a corrected title in corrected_title (Mode B). A correction creates a new proposal version; it must then be approved separately.
flag / commentFlag an uncertain case (reason required) or leave a comment. Neither publishes anything.
reopenReopens a decided proposal. Requires reviewer name and a reason.
Returning decisionsReturn the sheet or CSV. It is imported with tool/import_review_decisions.js, which matches each row to its exact proposal ID and version and never touches unrelated proposals. Rows for an out-of-date version are refused and reported.
Sheets1 Review instructions · 2 Proposal catalogue · 3 Original vs personalised · 4 Excluded passages · 5 Flagged passages · 6 Review decisions · 7 Coverage statistics · 8 All verses (classification of every canonical verse).
